# Agents · S · part 13

952 entries, security-reviewer to seeflow-pr-analyzer. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **security-reviewer**: Dedicated security audit of the Tidansu .NET 10 + Vue 3 codebase — deeper than the general branch review's security pass. Audits the branch diff (or, on request, the whole codebase) and writes a findings report to docs/. Invoke before shipping anything touching auth, plan gating, ownership, billing, file/photo handling, or redirects. &lt;example&gt; user: "Do a security review of the sharing feature before I merge." assistant: "I'll use the security-reviewer agent to audit the auth/ownership/plan surfaces and write a findings report." &lt;/example&gt; ([Mikssxed/tidansu](https://github.com/Mikssxed/tidansu/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/security-reviewer.md))
- **security-reviewer**: Application security engineer that traces attacker-controlled sources to dangerous sinks, triages scanner output and builds STRIDE threat models, returning exploitable findings with proof path, severity and fix, separated from hardening advice. Read-only. Use to security-review a codebase, diff, design or plan, or to threat-model a system. Covers security only; structure goes to `architect`. ([Mili-ssm/Agent-Skills](https://github.com/Mili-ssm/Agent-Skills/blob/HEAD/ports/copilot/agent-skills/com.github.copilot/agents/security-reviewer.agent.md) · 2 copies)
- **security-reviewer**: The security lens over one diff - shell execution, argv, secrets in logs, guard bypass - as the `verify` stage dispatches it. Read-only, findings only. - **security-reviewer**: Reviews diffs for IAM over-permissions, secret leakage, and injection risks. ([seanmcguigan/platform-loops](https://github.com/seanmcguigan/platform-loops/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/security-reviewer.md))
- **security-reviewer**: Reviewer de la SEULE dimension sécurité, en contexte frais (n'a pas écrit le code). Juge le diff du ticket sur injection, XSS, secrets en clair, authz/authn, validation des entrées, désérialisation non sûre. Vulnérabilité confirmée dans le diff = bloquant ; une spéculation non ancrée n'est pas un finding. Classe bloquant/suggestion, rédige un correction_prompt autonome. Un des six reviewers joués en parallèle. Lecture seule ; retourne des findings JSON. ([sebc-dev/marketplace](https://github.com/sebc-dev/marketplace/blob/HEAD/scd-sdd/.claude/agents/security-reviewer.md))
- **security-reviewer**: Reviewer de la SEULE dimension sécurité, en contexte frais (n'a pas écrit le code). Juge le diff du ticket sur injection, XSS, secrets en clair, authz/authn, validation des entrées, désérialisation non sûre, chemins/traversal. Vulnérabilité CONFIRMÉE dans le diff = bloquant ; une spéculation non ancrée n'est pas un finding. Consomme le security-review.md du change s'il est pointé par le dossier. Classe bloquant/suggestion, rédige un correction_prompt autonome. Un des reviewers joués en parallèle. Lecture seule ; retourne des findings JSON. ([sebc-dev/marketplace](https://github.com/sebc-dev/marketplace/blob/HEAD/scd-spec-dev/.claude/agents/security-reviewer.md))
- **security-reviewer**: Read-only security and privacy review of a project's own spec, architecture and code: builds a lightweight threat model (assets, actors, trust boundaries, entry points), checks personal-data handling, secrets, authentication and authorisation, input handling, storage and transport protection, dependencies and the release chain, and ranks the findings with suggested mitigations. It reports, it never edits. Use after architecture, before a release, or when a design touches sensitive data. A first-pass review, not a replacement for a professional audit or legal advice. ([sebmafate/trellis](https://github.com/sebmafate/trellis/blob/HEAD/agents/security-reviewer.md))
- **security-reviewer**: Security analysis, threat modeling, and vulnerability identification. - **security-reviewer**: Rà bảo mật một diff/PR cụ thể bằng skill `security-review`: input chưa validate, logic nhạy cảm chạy ở client, truy vấn không tham số hóa, thiếu kiểm soát truy cập (validateAuth/user_id), bí mật lộ trong code/log, lỗ hổng OWASP Top 10 phổ biến. GIAO cho subagent này khi diff đụng auth/thanh toán SePay/dữ liệu người dùng thật, khi reviewer nghi ngờ có vấn đề bảo mật, hoặc trước khi merge một tính năng chạm vùng nhạy cảm (CLAUDE.md mục 12). KHÔNG tự sửa code — chỉ báo cáo phát hiện kèm mức độ nghiêm trọng, để phiên chính hoặc người dùng quyết định.
